### [1] [Theory of Anomalous Diffusion and Superdiffusion in Integrable Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33274)
*Jitendra Kethepalli,Andrew Urilyon,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 可积系统在两点关联中可呈现扩散或 KPZ 标度，即便其电流全计数统计（FCS）与通常所关联的普适类不同；本文用保留可积自旋链散射结构的多组分粒子气体解释了这种分离。对 Δ>1，大粒子被可动浴场平流输运，FCS 呈高斯混合型并趋向 M-Wright 分布，可动组分导致有限时间圆化；在 Δ=1 各向同性点，所有粒子均可动且大粒子的电荷转移离散，FCS 由自洽泊松分布描述，主体近高斯、尾部依赖模型且非高斯。相反，两点关联由单个大示踪粒子的统计给出：Δ>1 时作布朗运动导致高斯扩散；Δ=1 时涨落流体力学证据表明动力学关联中涌现 KPZ Prähofer–Spohn 标度函数，来源于最大粒子与连续谱较慢粒子的有限耦合及其长程速度涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释可积系统中为何两点关联可呈现扩散或 KPZ 标度，而其电流全计数统计却与通常这些普适类所预期的形式不同，厘清二者为何可以分离共存。

Method: 采用一个保留可积自旋链散射结构的多组分粒子气体框架，将 FCS 与两点关联分别归因于不同统计对象：前者由多粒子电荷输运（Δ>1 用高斯混合/M-Wright，Δ=1 用自洽泊松分布）描述；后者由单个大示踪粒子的统计描述（Δ>1 布朗运动，Δ=1 用涨落流体力学导出 KPZ Prähofer–Spohn 标度函数）。

Result: 对 Δ>1，大粒子被可动浴场平流输运，FCS 为高斯混合型并在晚期趋向 M-Wright 分布，可动组分造成有限时间圆化；示踪粒子作布朗运动，两点关联呈高斯扩散。对 Δ=1，所有粒子可动且大粒子电荷转移离散，FCS 由自洽泊松分布描述（近高斯主体、依赖模型的非高斯尾部）；涨落流体力学证据显示两点关联涌现 KPZ Prähofer–Spohn 标度函数，源于最大粒子与连续谱较慢粒子的有限耦合及长程速度涨落。

Conclusion: 普适的动力学两点关联可以与依赖模型的电流统计共存；分离来源于散射结构（决定模型依赖的 FCS）与单个示踪粒子的长时动力学（决定扩散或 KPZ 普适关联）之间的不同，从而统一解释了可积系统中扩散/KPZ 关联与非常规电流统计并存的现象。

Abstract: Integrable systems can exhibit diffusive or Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) scaling in two-point correlations even when their current full counting statistics (FCS) differ from those usually associated with these universality classes. We explain this separation using a multispecies particle gas that preserves the scattering structure of integrable spin chains. For anisotropy $Δ>1$, large particles are advected by a mobile bath. The resulting FCS has a Gaussian-mixture form and approaches the M-Wright distribution at late times, with finite-time rounding due to mobile species. At the isotropic point $Δ=1$, all particles are mobile, and the charge transfer by large particles is discrete. A self-consistent Poisson distribution describes the FCS, with a nearly Gaussian bulk and model-dependent, non-Gaussian tails. By contrast, two-point correlations follow from the statistics of a large tracer particle. For $Δ>1$, it undergoes Brownian motion, yielding Gaussian spreading. At $Δ=1$, we provide evidence from fluctuating hydrodynamics for the emergence of the KPZ Prähofer--Spohn scaling function in dynamical correlations and trace it to the finite coupling of the largest particles to a continuum of slower ones, with long-ranged velocity fluctuations. Our framework shows how universal dynamical two-point correlations coexist with distinct model-dependent current statistics.

</details>


### [2] [Extrinsic thermodynamic geometry, symmetry, and the cost of constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33443)
*Jordan R. Sawchuk,David A. Sivak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发展了离散系统慢驱动摩擦张量形式主义下的外禀热力学几何，用于研究受约束控制器沿分布空间子流形驱动随机系统时的最优路径问题，并证明对称子流形上的最优协议在解除约束时仍保持平稳，且给出局部与全局最优性的判定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动随机系统在有限时间内会付出超额功，其大小取决于控制器能访问哪些系统参数、即分布空间中的哪些路径。当控制器受约束时，需要判断它是否被迫走上次优路径，而这本质上是一个子流形外禀几何的问题；此前缺乏适用于离散系统的系统化理论工具来在优化前通过几何对称性判定约束最优性的平稳性，也无法解释为何部分对称破缺在特定控制下才最优。

Method: 在慢驱动摩擦张量形式主义中，将平衡分布空间视为黎曼流形，控制约束定义子流形。作者为离散系统发展了子流形的外禀热力学几何：利用跃迁网络的对称性识别外禀曲率为零的子流形（对称子流形）；将垂直于对称子流形的环境曲率按对称破缺模式分解为独立贡献；并提出一个热力学比较定理，用对称协议沿线的极值曲率与其超额功作比较来廉价地检验局部极小性；在跃迁速率与控制参数满足凸性条件时，无需此类检验即可保证对称最优为全局最优。

Result: 对称子流形上的最小功协议在解除约束后超额功仍保持平稳（该平稳性在慢驱动之外也严格成立）；垂直方向的曲率量化了打破某一对称的热力学激励，并获得可操作含义；比较定理提供了局部极小性的廉价检验。理论成功证明四自旋 Ising 系统中一个五参数协议与十五参数全控制达到相同的全局最优，并解释了为何同一系统在局域场控制下部分对称破缺才是最优。

Conclusion: 通过发展离散系统的外禀热力学几何，可以在优化前依据跃迁网络对称性识别零外禀曲率子流形，从而判定约束最优协议在解除约束后的平稳性；借助曲率分解、比较定理及凸性条件可分别评估对称破缺激励、检验局部极小性并保证全局最优性，为受约束随机热力学控制提供了几何化与可操作的理论框架。

Abstract: Driving a stochastic system in finite time costs excess work; exactly how much depends on which system parameters, and hence which paths in distribution space, a controller can access. In the friction-tensor formalism for slow driving, the space of equilibrium distributions is a Riemannian manifold and constraints on a controller define submanifolds. Whether a constrained controller is forced onto suboptimal paths is a question of the extrinsic thermodynamic geometry of the submanifold, which we develop for discrete systems. We show that submanifolds with zero extrinsic curvature can be identified from symmetries of the transition network, before any optimization. A minimum-work protocol on such a submanifold is therefore stationary in the excess work even when the constraints are lifted. The stationarity of symmetric optima holds exactly, beyond slow driving. In the slow-driving regime, we show that the ambient curvature transverse to a symmetric submanifold determines whether nearby symmetry-broken protocols have lower excess work. This curvature, which separates into independent contributions from each pattern of symmetry breaking, thus acquires an operational meaning as a measure of the thermodynamic incentive to break a given symmetry. A thermodynamic comparison theorem provides an inexpensive test of local minimality that weighs the extremal curvatures along the symmetric protocol against its excess work. There is no need for such tests under certain convexity conditions on the transition rates and control parameters, which guarantee that a symmetric optimum is a global optimum. These results certify the global optimality of a five-parameter protocol previously observed to match 15-parameter full control of a four-spin Ising system, and explain why partial symmetry breaking is optimal for the same system under local-field control.

</details>


### [3] [The Phases of Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33566)
*Gerard McCaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出层级（hierarchy）的动力学相理论：深度决定一个能自我修正环境的N-规模群体能否在不过度修正的前提下纠偏。多数派的统计优势会使其响应按√N增长，反馈回环境后在临界环路增益处经倍周期（flip）分岔失稳；层级分层可抑制此失稳，所需深度随N对数增长。但深度自带成本：信号沿层级传递存在截止深度与代价随深度指数增长。由此形成“过平则振荡、过深则来不及纠错”的有界深度窗口（不稳定平相 vs 冻结惯性相）。作者将每层视为实空间重整化步骤，并与Transformer中的注意力机制建立联系，最后提出可观测的测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究群体在修正自身环境时如何避免“过度修正”引发的不稳定：Condorcet陪审团定理所示的统计优势（多数能检测微小偏差）同时使群体响应按√N放大，反馈回被评判的环境后会在临界增益处通过倍周期分岔破坏匹配状态。作者希望从动力学稳定性条件出发，理解组织经济学中已知的对数层级界限，并刻画“过平振荡、过深迟滞”之间的可行深度窗口。

Method: 建立一类“适应性正规形”（adaptive normal forms）模型，解析求出临界环路增益处的失稳（flip分岔）条件；用层级分层抑制失稳并推导所需深度的对数标度；分析信号沿层级链的传递，推导截止深度及到达代价随深度的指数增长；把每一层当作实空间重整化步骤，按“如何传递偏差”对聚合规则分类，并与Transformer中残差更新（token图上的扩散、深度即尺度）建立联系；最后在有限采样下考察相边界的可观测性并设计测量方案。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 无层级系统中，多数响应按√N增长，在临界环路增益处经flip分岔失稳。(2) 层级分层可抑制失稳，所需深度与N成对数关系，从而直接从动力学稳定条件复现组织经济学中的对数界限。(3) 深度超过有限值后，修正信号无法在截止时间前到达顶层；低于该值时代价随深度指数增长。(4) 可行深度存在于一个受上下界约束的有界窗口内：过平不稳定（振荡），过深冻结（惯性），二者之间是稳定可调控相位。(5) 聚合规则可按其偏差传递性质分类，连通到Transformer注意力机制。(6) 相边界可在有限采样中观测，并给出表征各相位的测量方法。

Conclusion: 层级深度存在一个由动力学稳定性决定的有界可行窗口：深度过浅则群体过度修正、经倍周期分岔失稳振荡；深度过深则信号到达顶层过迟、代价指数增长而失去可调控性。该框架将组织经济学中的对数层级界限、信号传递成本，以及Transformer中深度即尺度的观点统一为一个动力学相图，并可由有限采样实验观测验证。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical phase theory of hierarchy, in which depth decides whether a population of size $N$ that revises its own environment can correct it without overcorrecting. The statistics that let a large majority detect a small bias - the basis of the Condorcet jury theorem - also make its response grow as $\sqrt{N}$. Fed back into the environment it is judging, this sharpening destabilises the matched state through a flip bifurcation at a critical loop gain, which we obtain analytically for a class of models we term adaptive normal forms. We show that this instability can be attenuated by hierarchical layering, requiring a depth scaling logarithmically with $N$. This recovers known logarithmic bounds in organisational economics directly from dynamic stability conditions. Hierarchical depth carries its own costs however, and by analysing the signal chain transmission up the hierarchy we show that beyond a finite depth no correction reaches the top before its deadline, and that below it the cost of arrival grows exponentially with depth. A hierarchy that is too flat therefore oscillates and one that is too deep cannot be steered in time, leaving a bounded window of depth between an unstable flat phase and a frozen inertial phase. We further show that these results can be generalised by treating each layer as a real-space renormalisation step, classifying aggregation rules by how they transmit bias. This establishes a connection between these dynamic results to attention in Transformers, where a residual update is diffusion on the token graph and depth plays the role of scale. Finally we show these phase boundaries are observable in finite sampling, and propose measurements which may characterise the proposed phases in real adaptive systems.

</details>


### [4] [Universal Work Extraction from Individual Sequences by Finite--State Information Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33984)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究以温度 T 的热浴、功库及承载确定性有限字母序列的磁带为工作对象的信息引擎，证明了可提取功的逆界并给出其通用可实现性，从而把最大功与序列的块 Lempel–Ziv 复杂度联系到通用编码上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望建立信息引擎（自主棘轮级联）与通用（信源）编码之间的双向联系，从而把可提取功的极限与序列的熵/复杂度（而非仅平均熵率）精确刻画，并直接用编码论的逆界与可实现性结论来刻画引擎的最优性能。

Method: 把引擎建模为具有固定能级与细致平衡动力学的自主棘轮级联，每次处理 m 个磁带单元、内部状态有限；利用经验块熵给出可提取功的逆界，并证明可由能级不依赖序列 x 的引擎通用实现；通过“能级定义码”与“涨落恒等式蕴含码”建立与通用编码的双向对应。

Result: 证明了每符号最大可提取功为 kT[ln|X| − H̄(x)]，其中 H̄(x) 为序列 x 的渐近归一化对齐块熵，等于其块 Lempel–Ziv 复杂度；擦除 x 所需最小功为每符号 kT·H̄(x)；并由此得到：无记忆通用引擎的最优冗余由 Shtarkov 和给出。

Conclusion: 信息引擎的最优热力学性能可由序列的块熵/复杂度完全刻画，且与通用编码有严格的双向对应，因此通用编码的逆界可原封不动地应用于引擎设计，实现了热力学与编码理论的统一。

Abstract: We consider information engines that interact with a heat bath at temperature $T$, a work reservoir, and a tape carrying an individual (deterministic) sequence $x$ over a finite alphabet $\X$. An engine is a cascade of autonomous ratchets with fixed energy levels and detailed--balance dynamics, each processing segments of $m$ tape cells and having finitely many internal states. We prove a converse bound on the extractable work in terms of the empirical block entropies of $x$,and show that it is achievable \emph{universally}, by engines whose energy levels do not depend on $x$. The maximal work per symbol is $kT[\ln|\X|-\bar H(x)]$, where $k$ is Boltzmann's constant and $\bar H(x)$ is the asymptotic normalized aligned block entropy of $x$, which equals its block Lempel--Ziv complexity, and the minimal work needed to erase $x$ is $kT\bar H(x)$ per symbol. Underlying these results is a two--way correspondence with universal coding: energy levels define a code, and a suitably operated cascade extracts $kT$ times $\ln|\X|$ per symbol minus the code length; conversely, a fluctuation identity shows that every engine fixed in advance defines a code. Hence, converses of universal coding apply verbatim to engines; e.g., the optimal redundancy of memoryless universal engines is given by the Shtarkov sum.

</details>


### [5] [Exploring Physical Mechanisms Underlying the Emergence of Collective Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33988)
*Yitong Cai,Ruizhe Yan,Dongrun Jian,Jie Su,Jin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用非平衡景观-流理论与粗粒化映射方法，揭示Vicsek模型中集体序（鸟群）在外乘（矢量）噪声下呈突变式涌现，而在内乘（角度）噪声下呈类连续涌现；逆时针非平衡流通过熵产生率驱动景观重塑，可作为相边界定位与相变类型判别的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型是研究活性物质中由无序组分涌现集体序的典范系统，但密度诱导的涌现行为在角度（内乘）噪声与矢量（外乘）噪声之间是否存在差异、以及驱动相变的支配机制，此前很少被探索。

Method: 将非平衡景观-流（landscape-flux）理论与粗粒化映射方法相结合：通过景观的盆数演化刻画相变连续性，通过流与熵产生率刻画非平衡驱动力及其热力学起源。

Result: 内乘噪声下景观由单盆连续演化为双盆再回到单盆（类连续相变）；外乘噪声下双盆景观在临界相变处突然出现（突变相变）。逆时针非平衡流维持全局圆周运动并驱动景观重塑；平均流与熵产生率在涌现阈值附近急剧变化，并在共存区内呈现显著峰值。

Conclusion: 揭示了外乘噪声下存在的独特突变式涌现，阐明了密度诱导集体序涌现的动力学与热力学机制。该框架可推广应用于定位相边界、区分相变类型，并可拓展至其他非平衡集体系统。

Abstract: Emergence of collective order from disordered constituents is a hallmark of active matter, and the Vicsek model (VM) serves as a paradigmatic system for its study. However, whether density-induced emergence in the VM differs between angular (intrinsic) and vectorial (extrinsic) noise, and what mechanisms govern the transitions, remain largely unexplored. Remarkably, we uncover that collective order emerges from a disordered state in a continuous-like manner under intrinsic noise but abruptly under extrinsic noise. Combining nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory with a coarse-grained mapping method, we show that the landscape evolves continuously from a single basin to two and back to one under intrinsic noise, but the two-basin landscape appears abruptly under extrinsic noise across the critical transition to flocking. The counterclockwise nonequilibrium flux sustains global circular motion, providing the driving force for reshaping the landscape. The mean flux and entropy production rate change steeply near the emergence threshold and exhibit pronounced peaks within the coexistence region, identifying them as the dynamical and thermodynamic origins of emergence. Our findings reveal a distinct abrupt emergence under extrinsic noise and offer deeper mechanistic insight, practical applications (e.g., locating phase boundaries and distinguishing transition types), and extensibility to other nonequilibrium collective systems.

</details>


### [6] [Rotation curves of dwarf galaxies and dark matter as a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons: reconstruction and fits of the deformation profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33999)
*Mahnaz Maleki,Asef Kheirandish,Morteza Nattagh Najafi,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质为q变形玻色子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）的矮星系模型。在Thomas-Fermi极限下，均匀的变形参数不会改变标准BEC晕的密度轮廓；但在实际星系中，变形参数对内区（kr≲1）表现为压低、对外晕近似常数，从而可用两个额外参数（高斯压低）改进旋转曲线拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质主导的矮星系是检验自相互作用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚暗物质模型的敏感场所。标准BEC晕在Thomas-Fermi极限下密度轮廓为ρ∝sin(kr)/(kr)，旋转曲线形状仅由两个尺度参数固定，但该标准模型对矮星系旋转曲线的拟合存在较大残差，需要更灵活的模型。

Method: 作者考虑q变形玻色子的凝聚体，在Thomas-Fermi极限下推导变形对晕结构的影响，证明空间均匀的变形参数只通过化学势进入，因而保持标准BEC轮廓不变。随后引入随位置变化的变形参数：内区（kr≲1）χ被压低，外晕近似常数。用高斯函数对内区压低建模（新增两个参数），以旋转曲线拟合八颗矮星系，并以rms残差和贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）与标准BEC、Navarro-Frenk-White（NFW）及Burkert轮廓进行比较。

Result: 各星系的轮廓共享定性形状（内区压低、外区近似常数），但压低的深度与宽度不具普适性。高斯压低模型的旋转曲线拟合rms残差为0.6–2.6 km/s，优于标准凝聚体的1.6–8.5 km/s。按BIC，变形凝聚体在4颗星系中优于标准BEC、NFW和Burkert轮廓，在2颗中与三者最优者不可区分，在2颗中不受青睐。

Abstract: Dark-matter-dominated dwarf galaxies are a sensitive testing ground for models in which dark matter is a self-interacting Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC). In the Thomas-Fermi limit the standard BEC halo has the density profile $ρ\propto\sin(kr)/(kr)$, so that the shape of its rotation curve is fixed up to two scale parameters. Here we consider a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons. We show that, in the Thomas-Fermi limit, the deformation enters the structure of the halo only through the chemical potential of the condensate; consequently, a spatially uniform deformation parameter leaves the standard BEC profile unchanged. In every galaxy $χ$ is depressed in the inner region, $kr\lesssim1$, relative to the outer halo, where it is approximately constant in most cases: the standard condensate describes the outer halo, while the core requires a weaker deformation, i.e., $q$ closer to unity. The profiles share this qualitative shape, but neither the depth nor the width of the depression is universal. Modelling the depression by a Gaussian, with two additional parameters, we fit the rotation curves with rms residuals of $0.6$--$2.6$~km\,s$^{-1}$, against $1.6$--$8.5$~km\,s$^{-1}$ for the standard condensate. According to the Bayesian information criterion, the deformed condensate is preferred over the standard condensate, the Navarro-Frenk-White profile, and the Burkert profile in four galaxies, is indistinguishable from the best of them in two, and is disfavoured in two. We discuss the limitations of these results, in particular the absence of error bars in the data, the neglect of baryons, and the phenomenological status of a position-dependent deformation.

To address non-uniform angular and radial spacing in a circular interface, we assess a reconstruction procedure that weights slope minimization against medial interface position. We find that while spectral estimates were most effective in the original interfaces, this advantage did not persist after circular mapping. Abstract: We introduce a data-enhanced self-learning projective quantum Monte Carlo (PQMC) method that incorporates projective measurements from programmable quantum simulators into the guiding wavefunction of a ground-state projection. We demonstrate the approach for a two-dimensional Rydberg-atom array using restricted Boltzmann machine (RBM) and recurrent neural network (RNN) guiding wavefunctions. Experimental pretraining reduces finite-population effects and accelerates self-learning convergence, allowing unbiased estimation of ground-state properties to be obtained with fewer walkers and fewer self-learning iterations. The improvement is particularly pronounced for the RBM, while the more accurate RNN guiding wavefunction exhibits a smaller, though still observable, benefit from pretraining. Simulations across different system sizes suggest that the relative advantage of experimental pretraining may increase with system size. Improvements are also observed for the checkerboard order parameter, despite deviations of the experimental measurements from the equilibrium numerical results. Finally, finite-size scaling with self-learning PQMC without experimental pretraining yields the critical point and critical exponents of the disordered-to-checkerboard transition in agreement with previous numerical studies. Our results show that limited and imperfect experimental measurements can provide an effective initialization for self-learning PQMC, while imaginary-time projection combined with subsequent unsupervised learning progressively refines the guiding wavefunction toward the ground state.

Abstract: Entanglement asymmetry has emerged as a powerful probe of symmetry restoration and the quantum Mpemba effect. However, its conventional Rényi generalizations, defined as differences of Rényi entropies, are not monotones in the resource-theoretic sense. Here, we investigate an alternative family of asymmetry quantifiers based on sandwiched Rényi divergences, which satisfy monotonicity under symmetry-preserving quantum channels. Developing a two-parameter replica approach, we show how these quantities can be computed from charged moments and establish a remarkable correspondence with conventional Rényi asymmetries under the inversion of the Rényi index, $α\leftrightarrow 1/α$. This relation, exact for pure states, holds at leading order in the relevant scaling limits for homogeneous quantum quenches, including interacting integrable systems, and for Haar-random states. Applying these results to quenches from tilted ferromagnetic states, we demonstrate that the quantum Mpemba effect is remarkably robust, occurring throughout the entire family of sandwiched Rényi asymmetry monotones. We identify dynamical regimes in quenches from spatially inhomogeneous initial states where conventional Rényi asymmetries and genuine monotones exhibit qualitatively different behaviours. We also investigate a solvable toy model illustrating the possible dependence of Mpemba crossings on the Rényi index. Our findings establish a connection between conventional Rényi asymmetries and quantum resource theory, explaining their effectiveness in broad classes of nonequilibrium protocols while highlighting the additional information provided by genuine asymmetry monotones.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组装生长的多相薄膜常涉及金属相与氧化物相的共沉积，此时氧化物可能以化学计量分子形态存在于金属表面。定量理解这类分子氧化物在金属表面上的稳定性、结合与扩散行为，对于理解自组装机制至关重要，然而相关基础数据仍然缺乏。

Result: 分子态CeO2在所有Au表面上均比其原子组分解离态更有利；弯曲CeO2构型比线性构型更稳定（Ce位于hollow位、O位于top位）；结合能约为-1 eV或更弱时，该构型在室温以上存在的概率很低。电荷转移使CeO2分子在所有表面上均发生极化，但仅在Au(001)和Au(110)的强结合态极化更强。最终归纳出三种结合案例：案例1为对称键的化学吸附，案例2为非对称键的化学吸附，案例3为对称键的物理吸附；Au(001)和Au(110)上三种案例均存在，而Au(111)上仅存在案例2和案例3。

Conclusion: CeO2分子在不同Au表面上的结合行为可依据表面取向分为三类——对称化学吸附、非对称化学吸附和对称物理吸附；分子态CeO2整体更稳定且弯曲构型占优，弱结合态在室温下难以稳定存在，这些结果为理解金属-氧化物共沉积自组装过程提供了基础性认识。

Abstract: Many multi-phase thin films grown by self-assembly involve co-deposition of both metallic and oxide phases that grow into the same film. Under such conditions, the oxide species may be present in stoichiometric molecular form on the surface of the metallic phase. Quantifying the stability, binding, and diffusion of such molecular oxide states on metallic surfaces is critical for understanding self-assembly. In this work, we investigate the binding and stability of CeO2 molecules on Au surfaces using spin-polarized density functional theory calculations. Specifically, we examine how the configuration of the CeO2 molecule, its binding energy and binding free energy, structural stability, effective charge, and projected electronic density of states vary among various binding sites on Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) surfaces. Molecular CeO2 was found to be more favorable on all Au surfaces than its atomic constituents. It was also found that the bent CeO2 molecule configuration is more stable than the linear counterpart, with the Ce atom on the hollow site and the O atoms on the top sites. In addition, the configurations of the CeO2 molecule on Au surfaces with a binding energy around or weaker than -1 eV were found to have a low probability of existence above room temperature. Furthermore, the charge transfer was found to occur in such a way that the CeO2 molecule becomes polarized on all surfaces, but the polarization is stronger only for the strongly bonded states on Au(001) and Au(110) surfaces. Finally, three different cases of how the CeO2 molecule binds to Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) were identified: case 1: chemisorption with symmetric bonds, case 2: chemisorption with asymmetric bonds, and case 3: physisorption with symmetric bonds. These three cases of CeO2 binding are present on Au(001) and Au(110), but only cases 2 and 3 occur on Au(111).

### [13] [Magnetically Guided Sequential Multi-Filler Alignment Enables Anisotropic Heat Pathways in Stretchable Composites for Soft Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33381)
*Altynay Kaidarova,Viktor Naenen,Isidro Florenciano,Ruben Windey,Xiaoyan Qing,Laurens Snels,Juhyung Park,Tom Van der Donck,Jeroen Soete,David Seveno,Francisco Molina-Lopez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分级两步工艺策略，在超软弹性体复合材料中实现高通面导热与极低横向导电的各向异性解耦：先"对齐后共混"形成镍微粒垂直导热骨架，再渗透液态金属/镍混合物桥接颗粒间隙，最终获得 7.5 W/(m K) 通面导热、横向电导率低于 10⁻¹² S/m、杨氏模量约 160 kPa 的复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可拉伸贴肤电子器件需要兼具高通面导热、机械柔软性和横向电绝缘的热界面材料。但在颗粒填充复合材料中，这些性能要求通常相互拮抗：提高颗粒含量以缩短颗粒间距会不可避免地带来机械增强（变硬）和面内导电，而现有颗粒取向加工策略难以有效解耦这些多功能性。

Method: 提出分级两步工艺：第一步采用"先对齐后共混"（align-before-blend）方法，在空气中通过磁场对齐镍（Ni）微粒形成高有序、致密堆积的垂直导热骨架，由于对齐阶段没有粘性弹性体基体，故可处理大量颗粒；第二步将液态金属（LM）/镍混合物渗透进对齐骨架，以桥接颗粒间间隙、降低颗粒间热阻。

Abstract: Stretchable, skin-conformal electronics demand thermal interface materials that combine high through-plane heat transport with mechanical softness and lateral electrical insulation. In particle-filled composites, these requirements are typically antagonistic. Heat transport is governed not only by the intrinsic thermal conductivity of the fillers but, more critically, by thermal resistance at the interparticle interface. As the interparticle distance is reduced by increasing the particle content, mechanical reinforcement and in-plane electrical conductivity become unavoidable. The existing particle alignment processing strategies result in inefficient uncoupling of multifunctionality. Here, we present a hierarchical two-step process strategy to induce strong conductivity anisotropy in a soft composite. First, an align-before-blend process forms a scaffold of magnetically aligned nickel (Ni) microparticles in air before polymer infiltration. The absence of the viscous elastomer matrix during the particle alignment facilitates handling large amounts of particles, which assemble into highly ordered and densely packed vertical heat-conduction pathways. Second, a liquid metal (LM)/Ni mixture is infiltrated into the aligned scaffold to bridge interparticle gaps, minimizing interparticle thermal resistance. The composite reaches 7.5 W/(m K) through-plane thermal conductivity with lateral electrical conductivity below $10^{-12}$ S/m and ultrasoft mechanical compliance (approximately 160 kPa), enabling coupling layers in thermoelectric generators and finger-mounted heat-spreading pads for wearable technologies.

Abstract: Electron--phonon interactions govern superconductivity, electrical resistivity, optical absorption and electronic excitation lifetimes in solids. First--principles calculations require interpolating this coupling onto dense Brillouin--zone grids, traditionally using maximally localized Wannier functions that demand material--specific tuning. Here we show that a fixed basis of pseudo--atomic orbitals--automatically generated from pseudopotentials and implemented in the open--source code PAOFLOW captures the electron--phonon vertex with equivalent accuracy and no manual tuning. Computed from density--functional perturbation theory or displaced supercells, and incorporating long--range fields and spin--orbit coupling, the vertex matches established codes on identical inputs. We validate the method on lead, reproducing its phonon dispersion, superconducting gap, critical temperature, resistivity, photoemission kink and cyclotron masses, and on silicon, capturing phonon--assisted optical absorption, wafer emissivity and carrier mobilities. This parameter--free framework enables high--throughput, automated electron--phonon calculations across large materials databases.

Abstract: Ferroelectric tunnel junctions (FTJs) hold great promise for nonvolatile memory, neuromorphic computing, and other applications. Hf$_\text{1-x}$Zr$_\text{x}$O$_2$ (HZO) has become a leading candidate for the tunnel barrier due primarily to its compatibility with CMOS technology. Recent advances on HZO based FTJs have highlighted the importance of understanding the electrical transport through a ferroelectric tunnel barrier for fulfilling the technological promises of FTJs. In this paper we report investigation of HZO FTJs featuring a barrier of ultrathin HZO ($\leq$5nm) measured down to a temperature of 2 K. Direct quantum tunneling was found to dominate at low temperatures, along with contributions from trap-assisted tunneling. As the temperature increases, conduction by thermal activation takes over. Abstract: Large-scale pretrained machine-learned interatomic potentials keep growing more accurate as new models appear at a rapid pace, and more expensive: the current leader of Matbench Discovery as of November 2026 (F1 score 0.935) has $9.9\times10^7$ parameters and took 3.6 thousand GPU-hours to train. The ILMlip potentials presented here are optimised for speed as well as zero-shot accuracy. They are equivariant, conservative message-passing potentials in which every component is arranged for throughput: little work per pair of atoms and as much as needed per atom. Through ASE on one GPU, they run molecular dynamics up to about $10^3$ times faster than state-of-the-art message-passing potentials such as MACE (medium) and up to five times faster than NEP89 and DPA-4.1C. At the same time, six sizes with $1.4\times10^5$ to $2.8\times10^7$ parameters, pretrained on materials, molecules and solid acids, reach F1 scores of 0.75 to 0.91 on Matbench Discovery, placing the largest within 0.03 of the current leader. They therefore suit high-throughput, large or long simulations, and, being transferable, they are strong starting points for fine-tuning. Fine-tuned to six materials, the model of size S comes close to the ab initio force errors of a fine-tuned MACE, so it reaches reference-level accuracy at about 50 ns per atom and step (Cu fcc). Their components can be adopted separately, as a starting point for architectures with even better ratios of speed to accuracy. The ILMlip implementation is available at https://github.com/jhaens/ilmlip

</details>

Abstract: Protonic ceramic fuel cells (PCFCs) offer efficient energy conversion at intermediate temperatures, but their performance is often constrained by sluggish oxygen reduction kinetics at the cathode. Thermodynamic defect models incorporating hydration, oxidation, and hydride formation reactions are employed to estimate temperature-dependent proton concentrations. Our results suggest that increasing the Y content from BCFZ to BCFY increases proton diffusivity by 1.7% at 500 $^\circ$C. Under PCFC operating gas conditions, proton concentration and proton conductivity are predicted to increase by 3.8% and 5.3%, respectively, at 500 $^\circ$C. These increases are modest compared with the larger enhancements reported experimentally under reducing conditions, highlighting the importance of gas conditions in evaluating the effects of Y doping. Under low oxygen partial pressure conditions, we predict that the dominant proton transport mechanism shifts from hydroxyl groups (OH$_O^{\bullet}$) to hydride species (H$_O^{\bullet}$). Abstract: Electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS) conventionally considers classical Coulomb interactions between probe and material electrons, overlooking their fermionic exchange at relativistic energies. Here, we develop a relativistic theory of magnon EELS incorporating both magnetic dipole and electron exchange interactions. We show that the two channels leave distinguishable electron spin states, suppressing their interference in conventional EELS. However, spin-resolved detection erases this which-path information, revealing first-order interference despite the weak exchange amplitude. Remarkably, the same interference produces a left-right intensity asymmetry with a spin-polarized incident beam, without requiring outgoing-spin analysis. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KS-DFT 的自相互作用误差导致轨道能量对其自身分数占据产生非物理的非线性依赖，并造成带隙低估等问题。为满足广义 Koopmans 条件（GKC）并消除自相互作用，需要一种基于 Levy 约束搜索的轨道密度依赖的密度泛函理论。

Method: 基于 Levy 自旋密度泛函理论的约束搜索，将 Kohn-Sham 方程中的电子密度约束为轨道密度依赖，使总能泛函对轨道密度变化呈线性；具体做法是将 KS 乘性有效势 v_s[n] 对于某轨道 φ_i[n] 约束为依赖 n-n_i 的泛函 v_eff[n-n_i]，从而满足 GKC。

Result: 该方案满足广义 Koopmans 条件，消除电子自相互作用；相较于 KS-DFT，将 LDA 和 GGA 在基本带隙上的平均绝对误差改善约 1 eV。

Abstract: The self-interaction error (SIE) of Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT) leads to a non-physical, non-linear dependence of an orbital's energy on its own fractional occupation [Dabo et al., Phys. Rev. B, 82:115121, 2010]. The generalized Koopmans condition (GKC) ensures an atomic orbital's eigenenergy is independent of its fractional occupation$-$ and is free of self-interaction. The constrained search of the Levy spin-density-functional theory yields orbital densities and delivers the ground-state energy to generalize the Hohenberg-Kohn theorems. In this work, the electron density of the Kohn-Sham equations is constrained to be orbital-density dependent, with a total energy functional linear with respect to variation of its orbital densities. That is, the KS multiplicative effective potential $v_{\text{s}}[{n}]$ for an orbital $\varphi_i[{n}]$ is constrained to a functional $v_{\text{eff}}[{n - n_i}]$. The result complies with the generalized Koopmans condition (GKC). This approach improves LDA and GGA mean absolute errors (MAEs) in fundamental band gaps by $\sim$ 1 eV, with respect to KS-DFT. Equations free of electron self-interaction provide the basis for improvements in the accuracy, efficiency, and simplicity of density functional theories.

Furthermore, we demonstrate the existence of chiral edge modes with extended Fermi arcs around high-symmetry points. Abstract: The region of fcc stability in Fe-rich Fe-Cr alloys, the $γ$-loop, has an anomalous shape: at low temperatures, Cr addition initially stabilizes fcc, but above 8 at.% Cr it stabilizes bcc. At high temperatures, Cr always stabilizes bcc. This behavior reflects a delicate balance between magnetic, vibrational and configurational free-energy contributions that is challenging to capture from first principles. Gibbs free energy differences between fcc and bcc are calculated from 800 to 1800 K. Our calculations reproduce the experimental $γ$-loop and its compositional dependence, with near-quantitative accuracy for the $γ$-$δ$ transition. The $α$-$γ$ boundary, including its anomalous shape, is reproduced qualitatively, although transition temperatures are underestimated by approximately 200 K. Static calculations of magnetically ordered and disordered states provide an explanation: in ordered states, increasing Cr initially reduces the energetic preference for bcc over fcc, whereas in disordered states, Cr progressively stabilizes bcc at all compositions. Vibrational effects contribute to phase stability, while atomic short-range clustering at higher Cr content ($x \geq 0.15$) may explain the observed closing of the $γ$-loop from below. The simulations reveal finite Cr moments in the paramagnetic state, whose magnitudes depend on their local magnetic environment. The results demonstrate that the anomalous $γ$-loop arises from the interplay of magnetic energetics, magnetic disorder, lattice vibrations and chemical clustering, which must be treated on equal footing to capture phase stability from first principle

Abstract: Phonon dynamics determine a vast number of properties in the solid state, such as the heat capacity, thermal expansion, and thermal conductivity. Phonons are obtained through the dynamical matrix, which, in the frozen phonon approach, is calculated as a finite difference derivative of the analytical Hellmann-Feynman force. This approach unfortunately typically suffers from requiring very large supercells, even for modest $\mathbf{q}$-grids. The non-diagonal supercell method remedies this issue by determining the smallest supercell commensurate with a given $\mathbf{q}$-point, drastically reducing the supercell size, but in turn requiring a new supercell for each $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid. This introduces redundancy, however, since a single non-diagonal supercell may be commensurate with several $\mathbf{q}$-points. In this work I present a method to determine a minimal set of supercells, such that every $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid is commensurate with at least one supercell. I present an algorithm to solve the problem and prove that it yields the optimal solution, before summarizing the algorithm using pseudocode. The scaling of the number of supercells with the density of the grid is discussed. Numerical results on a variety of systems show the significant computational advantage of the method, which increases with the density of the grid.

Abstract: Anomalous transverse shifts in interface reflection are conventionally attributed to spin-orbit coupling or its pseudospin analogue. Here, we uncover a distinct geometric mechanism driven solely by an orbital torque arising from Fermi-surface anisotropy. During total reflection, an incident wave packet penetrates the anisotropic transmitted medium as an evanescent wave. There, the broken rotational symmetry about the interface normal exerts an orbital torque, and the accumulated angular impulse---the product of the torque and the dwell time---leads to a transverse spatial shift. Applying this mechanism to altermagnets without spin-orbit coupling, we show that the orbital torque is odd in spin. As a consequence, the two spin channels are deflected to opposite sides of the incident plane, giving an intrinsic spatial spin separation in altermagnets. The shift also inherits the angular symmetry of the altermagnetic spin splitting and is strongly enhanced near the onset of total reflection. Abstract: Air-sensitive chalcogenide films are routinely protected by a capping layer, which must then be removed, or contacted through, before electronic transport can be measured, destroying the surface the cap was meant to preserve. Terahertz time-domain spectroscopy requires neither, but using it quantitatively demands a transmission model in which cap, film and substrate are treated on an equal footing. We formulate the cap/film/substrate problem exactly using a transfer matrix with a time-gated substrate, and show that the intuitive construction in which each thin layer contributes an independent Fabry--Pérot factor fails for two adjacent thin films. In the thin-film limit we obtain a closed-form inversion together with a criterion for the error incurred by ignoring the cap. We apply the framework to Bi$_2$Te$_3$ films grown by molecular beam epitaxy on GaAs at three thicknesses, each prepared with and without a 25 nm Te cap. A Te-on-GaAs witness shows the cap to be optically indistinguishable from bare substrate, transmitting 1.00(4) of the substrate amplitude between 0.2 and 1.5 THz, which limits the error from neglecting it to 3.6 % for the 90 nm film, although the same estimate reaches 48 % at 25 nm. In this matched set, the capped films form an ordered series, monotonic in thickness in both conductance magnitude and lineshape, whereas the uncapped films do not. The capping layer therefore presents no obstacle to terahertz characterization, and in this set it coincides with a more reproducible film response.

Abstract: This chapter pays tribute to Giorgio Parisi by reviewing the influence of his work on spin glasses and replica symmetry breaking (RSB) on combinatorial optimization and artificial neural networks. Particular attention is given to rare regions of high local entropy and their role in algorithmic accessibility. Abstract: Atomic defects and disorder affect both the microscopic magnon excitations and the macroscopic physical properties of real-world magnetic materials. The rationalization of this relation has hitherto remained elusive, because of the intertwined challenges of simulating with quantum accuracy atomic- and nanoscale inhomogeneities and correlations, and their effect on the magnon spectral function determining observables such as spin stiffness. Here, we introduce a theoretical and computational framework to address these challenges. First, we develop the Coupled Optimization of SpIN Environment (COSINE) algorithm for efficiently relaxing the atomic structure and magnetic configuration of a solid with defects or disorder to a local energy minimum. Then, we discuss how to efficiently obtain exchange interactions accounting for local compositional, structural, and magnetic-moment inhomogeneities. Finally, we develop a paraunitary unfolding approach for Bogoliubov bosons, which allows us to predict the magnon spectral function in materials with any magnetic order, including systems with canting and full, partial, or no magnetic-moment compensation. We showcase our approach in a representative defective ferrimagnet for generality, and then combine it with state-of-the-art spin-informed machine-learning potentials to quantitatively predict how nonuniformities in the spatial distribution of vacancy defects control blurring of effective magnon bandstructures.

Abstract: Superconducting diodes convert nonreciprocal critical currents into current-polarity-selective switching between zero-resistance and resistive states. The same threefold symmetry dependence is observed in field-free current-induced magnetization switching in A1-CoPt films, indicating a common crystallographic origin for the superconducting and normal-state transport responses. Current- and temperature-dependent second-harmonic transport measurements are consistent with vortex-mediated nonreciprocity arising from an orientation-dependent interfacial edge landscape. We further exploit the magnetization-dependent diode polarity for proof-of-concept superconducting readout of electrically written magnetic states. These results establish crystallographic symmetry as an effective degree of freedom for engineering the superconducting diode effect, expanding the accessible materials space and providing a practical route toward field-free superconducting electronics.

Along two contours called Cooper surfaces, adding or removing a spin-singlet pair of electrons with opposite momenta costs no energy. The resulting logarithmic divergence of the pair susceptibility allows an arbitrarily weak pair-transfer attraction to stabilize a correlated nodal \(d\)-wave superconductor. Spin-resolved coincidence photoemission could test this pairing mechanism by resolving two zero-energy pair-removal ridges above the transition and a third along the underlying Luttinger surface below it.

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. The model describes the mutual interaction between an internal mechanical variable and temperature through temperature-dependent material properties and mechanical dissipation. The analysis combines a Volterra integral formulation, a spectral representation of the memory mechanism, thermodynamic decomposition of the hereditary power, local well-posedness analysis, linearization near equilibrium, and Laplace-transform asymptotic arguments. The spectral representation separates the energy stored in the relaxation modes from irreversible hereditary dissipation and establishes the thermodynamic consistency of the memory contribution. Under minimal assumptions on the constitutive functions and the kernel, local existence and uniqueness of solutions are obtained; for thermodynamically dissipative memory and a non-negative external heat source, positivity of the absolute temperature is preserved. The main result concerns the linearized dynamics near equilibrium: with the thermomechanical coupling kept unchanged, an exponential kernel produces exponential relaxation, whereas a power-law kernel with a long tail produces algebraic relaxation. A numerical comparison performed for the same set of dimensionless thermomechanical parameters illustrates these two asymptotic regimes. The results show that changing the class of the memory kernel alone can change the qualitative long-time relaxation law of the coupled system.

Abstract: We show that every second-order differential equation with time reflection describes the $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions of a local pair of oscillators that is invariant under exchange combined with time reversal. We determine when such a pair has a regular first-order Lagrangian, and when the solutions of the equation fix its coefficients. As the main example, we consider a recently studied equation whose solutions do not depend on its velocity coupling. In the corresponding pair this coupling acts only on the conserved total momentum, which vanishes for $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions, and each of the two conditions that single out the model places the pair at an exceptional point. When the spring and velocity couplings are both nonzero, the pair has no regular first-order Lagrangian, while the equation has one with second derivatives. Eigenphase ratios may thus reveal subtle finite-size effects in quantum-chaotic systems with particle-hole or chiral symmetry. Abstract: Schwinger-Dyson equations are a cornerstone of quantum field theory (QFT). Derived using integration by parts, they capture relations between correlators of different orders, forming an open, infinite-dimensional system. In zero-dimensional QFTs with polynomial actions, which is just a physical interpretation for multivariate Gaussian integrals with polynomial insertions, conventional Gröbner elimination can be used to express all higher-order correlators (moments) in terms of a fixed basis set. One consequence is a closed finite-dimensional system of differential equations for the coupling dependence of these `master' correlators.
  An important application of zero-dimensional QFTs is in counting discrete geometric structures (as in simplicial approaches to quantum gravity). For the problem of counting labelled regular graphs, applying this Schwinger-Dyson closure results in a computational breakthrough relative to the existing state-of-the-art: for 9- and 10-regular graphs on $n$ vertices, a finite-depth, finite-width recursive formulation is obtained, making it possible to compute the total graph counts in $O(n)$ arithmetic operations.

Conclusion: 基本对称空间具有自然的Banach对称空间结构，其几何量均可通过Krein自伴算子的平坦导数切向投影显式刻画，正平方根在等距与transvection中扮演核心角色。

Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. After recalling the equivalence of the associated Hilbert norms, we describe two global operator parametrizations and their relation. The space is realized as a Banach symmetric space under $m(J,J')=JJ'J$. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. A proof of their intertwining property in an earlier publication is corrected.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线自旋密度泛函理论需要超出传统共线 LSDA 的近似来描述任意方向的磁化及其空间变化。作者受 Eich 和 Gross 工作启发，旨在通过自旋螺旋波作为局部参考，构造并严格控制一种固定轴 TSGA 的误差，从而为同时刻画磁化方向及其旋度提供有理论保证的近似。

Method: 从减去 Hartree 项的大正则 Levy-Lieb 能量出发，以自旋螺旋波（SSW）作为局部参考态；构造具有恒定总密度和规定 SSW 磁化的电子气的热力学极限，得到单位体积能量 e_SSW，并在局部波矢 ∇φ（φ 为螺旋相位）处取值。当 φ 取磁化强度 m 的方位角时，近似简化为 E(ρ,m)≈∫ e_SSW(ρ(x),m(x),∇φ(x)) dx，其中 ∇φ 描述 m 绕第三轴的局部旋转，等于 (m₁∇m₂−m₂∇m₁)/(m₁²+m₂²)。在旋转框架下的自旋极化密度和局部波矢以 N^{1/3} 长度尺度变化时证明误差界。

Result: 该近似仅依赖总密度 ρ、磁化强度 m 及其一阶导数；当自旋极化密度与局部波矢在 N^{1/3} 尺度上变化时，得到 O(N^{5/6}) 的误差界（能量为 O(N) 量级）。将相位置零可得到适用于任意磁化方向的非共线 LSDA。

Conclusion: 作者证明了固定轴 TSGA 的定量误差界，并表明该近似在描述非共线磁化时具有严格的理论依据；同时通过相位置零建立了与非共线 LSDA 的联系，为非共线自旋密度泛函理论提供了可控的梯度型近似。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[
  E(ρ,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(ρ(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $ρ$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. Setting the phase to zero gives the noncollinear local spin density approximation (LSDA) for arbitrary magnetization directions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于梯度的参数模型训练视为阻尼粒子在损失势场中的运动，并借助给耗散系统赋予作用量原理的 Bateman-Galley 双倍自由度方法，希望用统一的哈密顿力学框架严格刻画反向传播及各类优化器，从而揭示反向传播的深层结构与几何/代数本质。

Result: 所有架构的层约束均为第二类约束；哈密顿方程配以 Dirac 括号可精确复现损失梯度的训练，尽管哈密顿量仅含网络输出的代价。激活与参数动量的 Dirac 括号即其 Jacobian。理论结论在整个双倍相空间成立而不仅限于物理极限。数值实验验证到机器精度，并且作者无需反向传播即可通过积分约束的 Dirac 流直接训练网络。

Conclusion: 反向传播可被理解为受损失载荷约束的动力学链（运动链）中的约束力，其代数结构对所用优化器保持不变；Nesterov 加速仅增加一层动量相关的约束，梯度流则是 Dirac 括号的分阶段构造。该哈密顿/约束框架为网络训练提供了无反向传播的新实现途径。

Abstract: Gradient-based training of a parametric model is the motion of a damped particle in the potential set by the loss. Using the Bateman-Galley doubling of the degrees of freedom, which gives dissipative systems an action principle, we write gradient flow, the heavy-ball method and Nesterov's accelerated gradient method as Hamiltonian systems. We show that, within this Hamiltonian description, backpropagation is Dirac evolution. The activations of a feed-forward network are treated as coordinates of phase space, and the layer equations as constraints. These constraints are second class for every architecture, and Hamilton's equations with the resulting Dirac bracket reproduce training with the exact gradient of the loss, although the Hamiltonian contains only the cost of the network output. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. These results hold in the full doubled phase space, not only in the physical limit. The network's part of the constraint algebra is the same for every optimizer, Nesterov's look-ahead adds one layer of momentum-dependent constraints that couple the two copies of the doubled system, and gradient flow is obtained by constructing the Dirac bracket in stages. We confirm these statements numerically to machine precision and train a network by integrating the Dirac flow of its constraints, with no backward pass in the computation.

</details>

Motivation: KP方程作为重要的可积非线性偏微分方程，其哈密顿形式已为人所知，但从作用量出发、系统性地推导其哈密顿结构（尤其是处理辅助场引入的奇异约束和周期场中的零模问题）尚需澄清，以便为后续量子化提供坚实基础。

Abstract: We derive the Hamiltonian formulation of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation from a singular Lagrangian by means of the Dirac-Bergmann algorithm. The Lagrangian contains an auxiliary field, which leads to an irregular secondary constraint. On the branch that reproduces the KP equation, all constraints are second class, and the Dirac brackets give the known Hamiltonian and Poisson operator. We confirm these results by the Faddeev-Jackiw method. Then, we consider fields periodic in the first spatial coordinate. In this case, the zero modes of the derivative operator lead to an additional secondary constraint on the mean of the potential. This constraint replaces the inverse derivative by its zero mean counterpart, and the resulting Hamiltonian is in accordance with the one of Reiman and Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii, up to normalization conventions. For decaying fields, the same condition is necessary for rapidly decreasing potentials to remain rapidly decreasing. We show that this constraint appears for a general class of KP-type equations. In addition, we classify the smooth solutions of the auxiliary-field action. We also relate our results to an existing quantization of the KP equation, for which the potential Lagrangian provides an action.

</details>

Conclusion: 热涨落消除了零温下层间自由平移的简并，系统在低温下必然选取十二种基态（所有四聚体共享同一宇称），形成盘状柱状序。该工作完全解决了 Heilmann–Lieb（1979）关于模型 III 在 μ+2a>0 时呈现液晶（柱状）序的猜想，并揭示了从零温层状结构到柱状序的涨落驱动机制。

Abstract: We study Model III of Heilmann--Lieb (1979), a lattice model for liquid crystals consisting of tetramers (plaquettes) on the cubic lattice at chemical potential $μ$, interacting via a hard-core repulsion and a lateral attraction of strength $a>0$ between coplanar, aligned tetramers. The model was conjectured to exhibit liquid-crystalline order at low temperature provided that $μ+2a>0$. We establish discotic columnar order throughout this regime, fully resolving the conjecture. Specifically, we prove the existence of exactly twelve extremal and periodic Gibbs measures, each breaking rotational symmetry, with one of three tetramer orientations dominant, and translational symmetry in exactly two directions, with one of four tetramer parities dominant within that orientation. Thus, the tetramers form one-dimensional columns arranged on a square lattice. This arrangement is not a priori evident from the zero-temperature picture, where energy only enforces a layered structure: each layer is fully packed and crystalline, but different layers are free to shift independently within their planes. The main novelty here lies in showing how thermal fluctuations lift this degeneracy in favor of the twelve ground states in which all tetramers share a common parity.

</details>

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ behavior of the optimal constant $κ_N$ in the two-dimensional fermionic many-particle Hardy inequality \[ \sum_{i=1}^N -Δ_i \geq κ_N^{(2)}\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq N}\frac{1}{|x_i-x_j|^2}, \] understood as a quadratic-form inequality on antisymmetric wave functions. The proof combines a dyadic Fefferman-de la Llave representation of the Coulomb kernel with separate estimates for squares of low and high particle occupation. A trace inequality controls the low-occupation regime, while sums of Neumann eigenvalues yield the sharp bound in the high-occupation regime.

### [71] [Pairing from anti-Hund's coupling under infinite Hubbard repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33286)

TL;DR: 研究在强关联极限（U→∞）下，声子介导的反洪特耦合 JA 如何在三角晶格双轨道 Hubbard 模型中产生超导。通过 DMRG 计算发现，在 1≤JA/|t|≤10 范围内存在稳健配对，最大配对能隙出现在 JA~|t| 处；Cooper 对位于键上而非格点，且在大 JA 极限下可由强耦合展开的受控理论描述。结果为超越 BCS 的强耦合超导体提供了证据，并与魔角扭曲双层石墨烯的可能应用相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子耦合是超导的天然来源，但在强关联材料中声子介导的相互作用通常远弱于格点 Hubbard 排斥 U。当 U 占主导时，声子是否以及如何仍能产生配对，是一个基本的开放问题。作者旨在厘清声子介导的反洪特耦合在大 U 极限下驱动配对的可能机制。

Method: 构建包含反洪特耦合 JA 的极小模型：即由光学声子介导的、两个轨道（或两个谷）之间的格点自旋交换，倾向于形成局域轨道间自旋单态。在 U=+∞ 极限下，三角晶格双轨道 Hubbard 模型（从 n=2 电子/格点的绝缘体掺杂离开）约化为四味（four-flavor）t-JA 模型。作者采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值计算，并结合 JA≫|t| 时以 t/JA 为小参数的强耦合展开，建立受控的有效超导理论。

Result: 1）DMRG 发现整个 1≤JA/|t|≤10 区间内存在稳健配对，最大配对能隙出现在 JA~|t|；2）Cooper 对局域在键（bond）上，而非来自 JA 提供的直接格点吸引，因为无穷大 U 已将格点配对排除出希尔伯特空间；3）当 JA≫|t| 时，正常态是第二类费米液体，其小费米面违反微扰 Luttinger 定理，强耦合展开（t/JA）为这种非传统金属的超导提供了受控有效理论。

Conclusion: 结果强有力地证明了一种超越 BCS 理论的强耦合超导体，由动能、声子介导的反洪特耦合与强 Hubbard U 三者之间的相互作用共同驱动。作者最后讨论了该机制在魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中的可能应用。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling is a natural source of superconductivity, but in strongly correlated materials the phonon-mediated interaction is often much weaker than the on-site Hubbard repulsion $U$. Whether, and how, it can still produce pairing when $U$ dominates is a basic open question. We address this question by studying a minimal model with an anti-Hund's coupling $J_A$: an on-site spin exchange between two orbitals or two valleys, mediated by optical phonons, that favors a local inter-orbital spin singlet. In the limit $U=+\infty$, the two-orbital Hubbard model on the triangular lattice, doped away from the insulator at $n=2$ electrons per site, reduces to a four-flavor $t$-$J_A$ model. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations find robust pairing throughout $1\le J_A/|t|\le10$, with the largest pairing gap at $J_A\sim|t|$. The Cooper pairs reside on bonds and do not come from a direct attraction provided by $J_A$, since infinite $U$ excludes on-site pairs from the Hilbert space. For $J_A\gg|t|$, the normal state is a second Fermi liquid whose small Fermi surface violates the perturbative Luttinger theorem, and a strong-coupling expansion in $t/J_A$ provides a controlled effective theory of superconductivity from this unconventional metal. Our results provide strong evidence for a strongly coupled superconductor beyond BCS theory, driven by the interplay of the kinetic energy, the phonon-mediated anti-Hund's coupling, and the strong Hubbard $U$. Finally, we discuss possible applications to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene.

</details>

Abstract: We perform a dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) + Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) study of collective spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$, using a bilayer two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model derived from density functional theory. Our study bridges the localized and itinerant descriptions of spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$.

Abstract: We study the ground state phase diagram of twisted bilayer graphene at magic angle as function of carrier density and the Coulomb interaction. Abstract: Recent Raman scattering experiments on bilayer nickelates have revealed pronounced spectral features in the spin-density-wave state, whose microscopic origin remains controversial, with interpretations ranging from two-magnon excitations to electronic transitions across SDW-induced gaps. In contrast, the dominant peak in the $B_{1g}$ channel emerges at the multiorbital energy scale $(M_{z^2}^2+M_{x^2}^2)^{1/2}$, reflecting the strongly hybridized character of the relevant electronic states. These distinct energy scales provide a unified interpretation of several Raman signatures observed in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ and offer a means of disentangling the orbital contributions to the SDW order parameters. Our results highlight that in contrast to the superconducting state, Raman peak positions in the SDW state cannot generally be identified with a single SDW gap and establish symmetry-resolved electronic Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of multiorbital density-wave order.

Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides (tTMDs) have emerged as a fascinating platform for many-body phases --- displaying fractional Chern insulators and superconductivity as a function of doping. Here, we give a microscopic derivation of a continuum model which accurately describes their bandstructure across a range of twist angles with angle-independent parameters. This angle-independent description rests on retaining both the monolayer valence and conduction bands, which introduce tunnelling paths through AB-stacked regions; upon downfolding to the valence band, these paths supply a substantial twist-tunable contribution to the interlayer coupling. We show that the model is a perturbation of a chiral model with a magic angle, explaining the near-ideal quantum geometry of the moiré bands. The model further produces a strongly different orbital magnetic moment to the single-orbital theory --- predicting an anomalous Landau level slope --- and a redistribution of charge between the layers, modifying the moiré ferroelectric potential.

Abstract: It has been emphasized recently that some extremal black holes are "critical" solutions, lying at the threshold between gravitational collapse and dispersion. We study the effect of quantum gravity on critical solutions for the collapse of a thin shell of charged dust. We consider the Hamiltonian that generates the shell equation of motion with respect to the exterior Killing time and coincides with the ADM energy on-shell. Near criticality, this Hamiltonian is shown to be canonically equivalent to that of a Liouville theory with negative potential. After quantization, this model predicts that a collapsing shell has a non-trivial probability of undergoing a quantum mechanical bounce when its energy above extremality is comparable to the "breakdown scale". The bounce probability approaches unity at extremality. Hence this model predicts that the critical surface in the classical theory is thickened quantum mechanically into a "critical layer". The probability density function for the black hole energy is suppressed below the breakdown scale in the same way as found recently by Turiaci and Wu.

</details>


### [86] [The black hole IR triangle for scalar QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33271)
*Fabiano Feleppa,Nava Gaddam,Nico Groenenboom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在拉伸的史瓦西视界上于树级构造了标量QED的黑洞红外三角形。软荷是近视察边角项，记忆是规范不变辐射场沿生成元的零模，由穿过视界的电荷流通过高斯定律固定。残余规范变换的Noether流因带电物质双向穿越视界而不守恒；扣除物质部分后的流即软荷，生成大规范变换，其Ward恒等式等价于每个辐射分波中的软光子定理。与零无穷远的最大区别在于拉伸视界上场的两个径向分支是独立的边界数据，导致硬荷是径向流、软因子是每条腿的符号。端点数据给出两个边界可观测量（时间积分的横磁、横电）；散射辐射场只贡献前者并抵消库仑记忆的变化，故软荷守恒。文中还讨论了红外发散、外态穿衣以及记忆在Hartle-Hawking态中的随机行走行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将零无穷远处建立的红外三角形（软荷-软定理-记忆的等价结构）推广到黑洞视界情形，即在拉伸史瓦西视界上为标量QED构造并验证软荷、软光子定理与记忆之间的对应关系，并揭示视界几何带来的新特征（两个独立径向分支）及其对软定理、记忆和Hawking辐射的影响。

Method: 在近视察麦克斯韦场的协变相空间中，把软荷定义为边角项，记忆定义为规范不变辐射场沿生成元的零模，并用高斯定律联系二者。通过扣除带电物质贡献的Noether流得到软荷，由其Ward恒等式导出每个辐射分波的软光子定理；软定理分别从近视Feynman规则和（其不控制发射时）场方程领头阶推导。进一步用两个径向分支的端点数据定义时间积分的横磁、横电两个边界可观测量，分析散射辐射场对二者的贡献及软荷守恒，并讨论红外发散、外态穿衣和Hartle-Hawking态中记忆的行为。

Result: 在拉伸视界上构造了完备的软荷、软定理与记忆对应的红外三角形。不同于零无穷远，视界上的场有两条独立径向分支：硬荷成为径向流，每腿按径向运动方向赋权，软因子为每腿一个符号。两个边界可观测量中，散射辐射场只贡献于时间积分的横磁场，并恰好抵消电荷的库仑记忆变化，从而保证软荷守恒；穿越视界的电荷则同时进入两个可观测量。所导出的逐分波软光子定理对所有辐射腿成立，软荷守恒成立。

Conclusion: 标量QED在拉伸史瓦西视界上存在完整的红外三角形，其结构与零无穷远情形平行，但因视界拥有两条独立径向分支而出现定性差异（硬荷为径向流、软因子为符号、两个边界可观测量、辐射只修正横磁分量等）。这些差异确保了软荷的守恒，并深刻影响记忆在含Hawking辐射的量子态中的演化，使其表现为由Hawking辐射驱动的随机行走。

Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. In the covariant phase space of the near-horizon Maxwell field, the soft charge is a corner term. The memory is the zero mode of the gauge-invariant radiative field along the generators. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 所提出的OPE框架与2d伊辛模型双磁性杂质短程势的DMRG数值结果高度吻合，凝聚量的DMRG结果也与可积性精确值一致；多个可解模型进一步验证了框架的普适性与不同行为特征。

Conclusion: 该框架把熟悉的局域算子OPE推广到平行线缺陷的融合，能逐阶固定缺陷相关函数的融合极限，仅剩融合缺陷上的非微扰凝聚系数，并得到数值与精确解的印证。

Abstract: We discuss the fusion of parallel line defects in theories defined by relevant deformations of a conformal fixed point. We propose an operator product expansion to describe this limit in terms of a Wilsonian effective action whose coefficients are computable in conformal perturbation theory around the UV. This allows one to fix, order by order, the fusion limit of correlation functions involving the defects, up to non-perturbative coefficients associated to correlation functions (condensates) on the fused defect. It thus generalizes the familiar OPE of local operators in QFT. As a non-perturbative test of our proposal, we consider the short-distance potential between two magnetic impurities in the $2d$ Ising model. We find excellent agreement between the OPE framework and DMRG extractions of the potential and condensates (the latter also computed exactly with integrability). In addition, we consider a number of solvable models to illustrate different features of the framework.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a neural realization of massive scalar field theory on Euclidean anti--de Sitter space and its holographic boundary observables within neural network field theory (NN--FT). We construct Fourier--Bessel cosine neurons whose covariance reproduces the regulated massive AdS propagator exactly at finite network width, with the Gaussian field theory recovered in the infinite-width limit.

The first implements a prescribed interaction by deforming the neural parameter measure, while the second generates a local quartic interaction from the statistics of auxiliary binary neurons. Applying the BDHM prescription directly to the neural ensemble, we recover the scalar bulk-to-boundary propagator, the conformal boundary two-point function, and the leading connected four-point contact Witten diagram.
  We further show that distinct neural architectures can reproduce the same leading boundary correlators while defining inequivalent bulk theories. These results establish a framework for studying interacting scalar holography within NN--FT and clarify the role of neural architecture in encoding bulk dynamics and boundary observables.

Abstract: Type-IIB string compactifications typically give rise to a significant number of RR-photons. These are generically massless and do not possess any light charged states, making them `superhidden'. However, since supersymmetry is present at least at some high scale, corresponding `dark photinos' are also predicted. The kinetic mixing of the photons together with the SUSY breaking mass terms of the photinos makes the latter observable. We identify geometries in which the mixing is induced by Wilson lines and show that their expectation values are generically non-zero. As a result, most of the dark photinos, which are lighter than the visible sector SUSY breaking scale, mix significantly with the bino and hence couple to the Standard Model sufficiently strongly to leave potentially observable traces. In particular, volume modulus and inflaton decays generically induce a cosmological abundance of dark photinos that can explain dark matter. We find that the `natural Photinoverse dark matter parameter space' is already accessible to current experiments, notably gamma ray observations, which therefore have discovery potential in the near future.

Abstract: We derive the dimensional reduction of 10D super Yang-Mills theory on a general closed 6D spin manifold using spectral decompositions for the higher-dimensional gauge field and gaugino, keeping the infinite towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) modes in the lower-dimensional theory. Abstract: In this work, we study local operator quenches in thermal two-dimensional conformal field theories with complex-time evolution, which can be viewed as an effective description of conditional dynamics in a system interacting with an environment. In contrast to quenches of the vacuum, the thermal initial state allows several conformal families to participate in the evolution. We show that their competition can produce cascades of approximately homogeneous energy plateaux, with successive plateaux approaching the energies of different primary states. Using conformal Ward identities on the cylinder and torus, we determine the local energy density and illustrate this behavior in free-field theories, the Ising model and Wess--Zumino--Witten models. We develop a cold-limit spectral description in which the dominant primary channel determines the height of each plateau, while conformal dimensions and operator matrix elements govern the transitions between them. For a fixed compact unitary CFT, we show that only finitely many primary channels can contribute to the leading plateau sequence. Finally, we discuss the large-central-charge regime, relating light-operator plateaux to few-particle sectors in thermal AdS and examining the limitations of this picture for heavy quenches.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Love 数的界在牛顿星体中精确成立，但对致密星体失效，因为其假设对任意快速潮汐的响应为零或非负，而广义相对论中该响应为负。作者希望借助表面阻抗这一由内部唯一决定、编码完整潮汐响应的物理量，建立不依赖重整化方案与尺度的通用约束。

Method: 作者以物体表面阻抗为核心工具，引入两种稳定性物理性质作为约束：无损性（不耗散能量）和被动性（返还能量不超过所接收能量）。在广义相对论框架下，通过对阻抗施加这些约束，在任意重整化方案及任意尺度上推导 Love 数与 QNM 的界限。

Result: 1）无损体：每个动力学 Love 数均有上下界，此界在广义相对论中精确成立；QNM 被排除在由质量与半径确定的复频率平面区域之外，且已知 Love 数越多，禁区越大；反之，给定低阶 Love 数，QNM 可给出每个 Love 数的下界。2）被动体（视界尺度，如黑洞）：静态 Love 数落在一个窗口内，当物体表面趋近视界时，该窗口收缩至消失的黑洞值。

Conclusion: 通过表面阻抗对无损性与被动性的约束，可以统一且精确地界定致密天体（包括黑洞）的 Love 数与似正规模，克服了以往仅适用于牛顿星体的界在强引力情形下失效的问题，并为潮汐响应与 QNM 之间建立了双向约束关系。

Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. We derive bounds on Love numbers and quasinormal modes from constraints on the impedance that follow from either of two properties of a stable body: it is lossless, meaning that it dissipates no energy, or it is passive, meaning that it never gives back more energy than it has received. For a lossless body, every dynamical Love number is bounded from above and below, exactly in general relativity, in any renormalization scheme and at any scale. Earlier bounds are exact for Newtonian stars but fail for compact stars, because they take the response to arbitrarily fast tides to be zero or non-negative, whereas in general relativity it is negative. The quasinormal modes of a lossless body are also excluded from a region of the complex frequency plane set by the mass and radius. Each known Love number enlarges the excluded region. Conversely, the quasinormal modes bound each Love number from below, given the lower ones. For a passive body of horizon scale, such as a black hole, the static Love number lies in a window that closes onto the vanishing black-hole value as the body's surface approaches the horizon.

</details>

Abstract: According to the conjecture of Belinski, Khalatnikov and Lifshitz, general relativity gives rise to chaotic dynamics in the vicinity of generic spacelike singularities. However, no vacuum black hole has been shown to exhibit this behaviour. Here we construct stationary, asymptotically locally AdS$_5$ black holes with regular, triaxially squashed horizons and causal exteriors, whose homogeneous interiors realize the cosmological billiard of generic five-dimensional vacuum gravity. We identify a simple algebraic condition on the horizon data that is necessary for the interior billiard to be chaotic, and show that solutions with regular horizons locally access an open set of billiard trajectories.

</details>
